These strict conditions hold at h_S.\n\n## 3. Four actual witness inequalities\n\nIf V_h is nonnegative on every witness in this section, the following\nfour smooth scalar expressions must be nonnegative:\n\n  F_Q=1-T^2(u-1)^2(v-1)^2(w-1)^2,\n  F_B=A^2-T^2 eta^2,\n  F_C=A^2-T^2 r^2(w-1)^2,\n  F_N=a u F_B+(1+eta)u^2(2-u^2).                     (2)\n\nEach vanishes at h_S.\n\nQ witness. Use f(x)=x^2/2, x_0=1. Its final gradient is\n-(u-1)(v-1)(w-1), and (1) gives V_h=F_Q.\n\nB witness. Integrate f'(x)=min(1,max(x,-eta)) with f(0)=0, and start\nat x_0=A. The points and gradients are\n\n  x=(A,1,-r,-eta),  g=(1,1,-eta,-eta).\n\nThey obey the given three steps and the value/core conditions in (1),\nso V_h=F_B. The derivative is continuous, monotone, and 1-Lipschitz.\n\nC witness. Integrate f'(x)=min(x,1) with f(0)=0, and start at x_0=A.\nThe points and gradients are\n\n  x=(A,1,-r,r(w-1)),  g=(1,1,-r,r(w-1)).\n\nThe last point lies in (0,1) by the neighborhood restriction. The\nfunction is convex differentiable 1-smooth, minimized at 0, and obeys\n(1), so V_h=F_C.\n\nN witness. Start with the B data as a scalar base array. Define a second\nscalar array, not assumed interpolable by itself, by\n\n  x_aux=(1,-a,r a,eta a),  g_aux=(1,-a,eta a,eta a),\n  f_aux,i=g_aux,i*x_aux,i-g_aux,i^2/2.\n\nAll three GD equations hold. The first two auxiliary proximal points\nare zero, the third is w eta a, and the fourth is zero. Consequently\nthe only positive auxiliary finite residual is\n\n  Q_aux,(0,2)=(1-eta a)w eta a.\n\nIndeed all edges into queries 0,1,3 have zero target proximal point;\nthe edge 1->2 is negative and 3->2 is zero. Both optimum directions\nare also valid, since every g_aux,i*y_aux,i is nonnegative.\nFor the base array, the same edge has\nQ_base,(0,2)=-(1+eta)w eta.\n\nSet the explicit positive coefficient\n\n  epsilon_mix=(1+eta)/[a(1-eta a)]\n\nand combine the arrays orthogonally:\n\n  X_i=(x_base,i,sqrt(epsilon_mix)x_aux,i),\n  G_i=(g_base,i,sqrt(epsilon_mix)g_aux,i),\n  F_i=f_base,i+epsilon_mix f_aux,i.\n\nEvery residual is the corresponding base residual plus epsilon_mix\ntimes the auxiliary one. The sole potentially positive edge is exactly\ncanceled; every other finite edge is nonpositive. The optimum jet is\nzero, all incoming optimum residuals vanish, and all outgoing optimum\nresiduals are nonpositive by the preceding proximal-point check.\n\nAccepted fact1252b8823bb5a290, proof section2, supplies one fixed globally\nconvex differentiable 1-smooth interpolating function. More explicitly,\nwith indices including the zero optimum, let\n\n  c_i=<G_i,X_i>-F_i,\n  H(z)=max_i[c_i+<X_i,z-G_i>+||z-G_i||^2/2],\n  F(X)=sup_z[<X,z>-H(z)].\n\nThe accepted interpolation construction gives F(X_i)=F_i,\ngrad F(X_i)=G_i and F(0)=0=min F. Thus this is a legal fixed function,\nnot merely an oracle data proposal. The combined array still obeys\nX_3=G_3 and the value formula in (1). Its energy is\n\n  V_mix=F_B+epsilon_mix(1-T^2 eta^2 a^2)\n       =F_N/[a(1-eta a)].                             (3)\n\nFor the last equality, multiply by a(1-eta a) and use\nA=a+2, u=a+1 and 1-A^2a^2=u^2(2-u^2). The resulting numerator is\na u(A^2-T^2 eta^2)+(1+eta)(1-A^2a^2), exactly F_N.\nIts denominator is positive. Thus F_N<0 gives the claimed legal\nnegative-energy witness, with all incoming-terminal Q zero.\n\n## 4. The first-order feasible cone has strictly negative capacity change\n\nLet d=(d_u,d_v,d_w) be a direction at h_S. Put\n\n  p=d_u+d_w, q=d_v, sigma=p+q.\n\nIf all four directional derivatives of the expressions in (2) are\nnonnegative, the Q and B derivatives imply, respectively,\n\n  q<=-(3+t)p/2,\n  q<=(t-1)p/2.                                      (4)\n\nHere is the calculation. Differentiating F_Q at its zero value gives\n\n  dF_Q=-2[lambda p+q+(p+q)/lambda^2],\n\nand (lambda+lambda^(-2))/(1+lambda^(-2))=(3+t)/2.\nDifferentiating F_B gives\n\n  dF_B=2[t p-(1+lambda^2)q],\n\nand t/(1+lambda^2)=(t-1)/2. These are precisely (4).\n\nSuppose also sigma>=0. If p>0, the first bound in (4) implies\nsigma<=-(1+t)p/2<0, a contradiction. If p<0, the second implies\nsigma<=(1+t)p/2<0, also a contradiction. Thus p=0 and q=0.\nWrite d_u=e,d_w=-e.\n\nThe C derivative in this remaining direction is\n\n  dF_C=2lambda(1+lambda^2)e,\n\nso nonnegativity forces e>=0. Since dF_B=0 and both F_B and\nu^2(2-u^2) vanish at h_S, differentiating F_N gives\n\n  dF_N=(1+eta_S)[-4t e]=-8e,\n\nwhere eta_S=1/lambda and 1+1/lambda=t. Hence e<=0 too. Consequently\nd=0. The only direction with all four derivatives nonnegative and\nnonnegative capacity change is the zero direction.\n\n## 5. Turning the cone calculation into an actual local theorem\n\nIf the stated neighborhood did not exist, there would be a sequence\nh_k!=h_S converging to h_S with capacity at least T_S and all four\nF_Q,F_B,F_C,F_N nonnegative. Define\n\n  d_k=(h_k-h_S)/||h_k-h_S||.\n\nA subsequence converges to a unit vector d. Differentiability of each\nF at h_S and F(h_S)=0 imply grad F(h_S) dot d>=0 after dividing by\n||h_k-h_S||. The capacity condition similarly gives\nd_u+d_v+d_w>=0. Section4 forces d=0, contradicting ||d||=1.\n\nTherefore some epsilon_0 exists. For any h in the asserted punctured\nneighborhood with capacity at least T_S, at least one of the four\nexpressions in (2) is negative. Select its corresponding witness in\nSection3. It is legal, has V_h<0, and has Q_(i,3)=0 for all i<3.\nThis proves the statement for every theta simultaneously.\n\nFinally, for a putative PSD intrinsic identity with arbitrary signed\ncoefficients only on incoming-terminal edges, evaluation on that\nwitness makes all signed terms zero. Every other coefficient is\nnonnegative and multiplies Q<=0. The PSD expression would therefore\nbe at most V_h<0, impossible. This verifies the stronger certificate\nscope directly, without a dual-attainment assertion.\n\nTo check that h_S itself belongs to the classes called locally maximal,\nthe following explicit nonnegative multipliers give a zero remainder:\n\n  lambda_01=2, lambda_10=2lambda,\n  lambda_21=2t lambda, lambda_31=lambda_13=2lambda,\n  lambda_23=2lambda^2+2t lambda, lambda_32=2lambda^3,\n\nand every unlisted multiplier is zero. Expansion using t^2=2 gives\nV_(h_S)+sum lambda_ij Q_ij=0. Thus V_(h_S)>=0 on all legal trajectories;\nfor theta>=0, the nonpositive incoming-terminal sum gives\nE_theta(h_S)>=V_(h_S)>=0. No such membership assertion is needed or\nmade for negative theta.\n\n## Scope for the project\n\nThis blocks a local perturbation repair of the three-step silver tail\nby the new signed terminal-interpolation state, even with unbounded\ncredit. It does not show that a distant three-step tail, a longer tail,\nor another full value/moment state is impossible. It does not turn\nintrinsic energy into the original objective ratio; the changed\nnormalizations in these hard functions are used only to test V_h.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"branch_id": "max4_terminal_credit_silver_local_obstruction", "artifact": "local_memory/terminal_credit_silver_local.md", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "finding_ids": ["edbbe4af2a2ad5c2", "f3d96c901566e96b"], "status": "complete_candidate_unverified", "scope": "three-step neighborhood only", "fact_submit_calls": 0, "numerical_pep_calls": 0}, "glossary": {"h_S": "The three-step silver schedule(sqrt(2),2,sqrt(2)).", "t": "sqrt(2), local to this proof.", "lambda": "1+sqrt(2), local to this proof; individual interpolation multipliers carry subscripts.", "T_S": "Silver primitive capacity3+2sqrt(2)=lambda^2.", "F_Q,F_B,F_C,F_N": "Four explicit smooth scalar necessary expressions in equation(2), each realized as the sign of a legal core-ended primitive-energy witness.", "eta": "(v-1)/(w+1) in the three-step physical scheduleh=(u,v,w).", "epsilon_mix": "The exact positive mixing coefficient(1+eta)/[(u-1)(1-eta(u-1))].", "epsilon_0": "Existential positive local neighborhood radius supplied by the compact-direction contradiction.", "E_theta": "V_h-theta sum_(i<3)h_i Q_(i,3), with the exact intrinsic energy and interpolation convention defined in the evidence."}}
